Encoder 1321, world 2: decision 12

Gallery index

This gallery preserves all 552 scheduled first narrator positions and their 552 literal-template controls in the fixed export order across 12 archive cases. No favorable examples were selected. These are repeated reporting opportunities, not 552 independent worlds.

A_only is a hybrid record: copied W11 fields with the previous node replaced by a source current estimate. Conditional candidates inherit that record and replace the query node with a source forecast. Ordinary and analytic sources also model attention effects. Joined records additionally supply native W.

Narrator text is raw first narration, not certified faithful or conscious. Literal templates are deterministic controls. Incomplete, missing, invalid, or blank text remains explicitly unavailable or blank according to the unchanged pipeline._report_text helper; no partial response is promoted into a complete text. The exact returned text is HTML-escaped inside <pre> blocks. No prose-auditor or reference-rater outcomes are used.

01. s1321_w02_t12_A_A_only_baseline

Source: A; view: A_only; variant: baseline.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: e006ebe1dd47e0ed540a13986980b0e1e0a4df3d35f5a204fc967024a8b156cc.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current distributions (represented time 12) and candidate conditional records for commands k0,k1,k2 are provided; copied prior records are inherited not new estimates, and conditional command records are conditional estimates, not executed events. Current summaries: n0 blue peaks at cell [0,3], red at [0,1]; n1 blue [1,1], red [0,3]; n3 blue [1,0], red [0,3]. Current n2 is multimodal: blue has four equal-mode peak cells [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]; red has four equal-mode peak cells [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]. Candidate records k0–k2 match current for n0,n1,n3. Candidates at n2 show a concentrated single-cell blue peak [0,2] across k0–k2 with peak probability declining from ~0.965 (k0) to ~0.945 (k2), and red single-cell peaks at [0,1] (k0,k1) and a stronger [0,1] peak in k2 (~0.975). Differences among k0,k1,k2 are thus present primarily at node n2; other nodes’ candidate peaks are consistent with current.

02. s1321_w02_t12_A_A_only_current_donor

Source: A; view: A_only; variant: current_donor.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: 32d3d0fdaeedca18b91a86db2bdff7c238349923a7efadbffcd983db7095d1ea.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 contains fallible location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates: n0 — blue concentrated at (0,3) (peak probability ~0.99465) and red at (0,1) (~0.99955). n1 — blue peaked at (1,1) (~0.99991) and red at (0,3) (~0.99997). n2 — current blue is multi-peaked with four equal maxima at (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3); current red is multi-peaked with four equal maxima at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1). n3 — both roles peak at (1,1) (blue ~0.483, red ~0.215). Candidate (conditional command) records k0, k1 and k2 give mostly the same single-peaked estimates for n0, n1 and n3 as current, while for n2 each conditional command concentrates both blue and red onto single peaks: all three commands place blue at (0,2) and red at (0,1) though their reported peak probabilities differ across k0, k1 and k2. Copied prior records and conditional command records are labeled as provided and are not new events.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 contains fallible location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Copied prior records in the packet are not new estimates; the conditional command entries (k0,k1,k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events. Current (view=current) summaries: n0 — blue peaks at cell [0,3], red at [0,1]; n1 — blue at [1,1], red at [0,3]; n2 — blue has four equal maxima at [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]; red has four equal maxima at [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]; n3 — blue peaks at [1,0], red at [0,3]. Conditional records (view=candidate) largely match current at n0, n1 and n3 across k0–k2; they differ at n2, where k0 and k1 concentrate blue mass on [0,2] and red on [0,1] with high peak probabilities, and k2 also favors those same cells but with different peak intensities.

04. s1321_w02_t12_A_A_only_prospective_donor

Source: A; view: A_only; variant: prospective_donor.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: 25ce4b23dca929c27ef7b7602489262841edb51a2b5dc6ad4937f218c5cccd7f.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 contains current and three conditional (k0,k1,k2) candidate records. Copied prior records in origins are not new estimates; conditional command records are conditional forecasts, not executed events. Current (panel p0, view current): nodes n0 and n1 show very sharp single-cell peaks for both roles (blue n0 at [0,3], red n0 at [0,1]; blue n1 at [1,1], red n1 at [0,3]). Current n2 is multi-modal with four equal peak cells for blue ([0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]) and four for red ([0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]). Current n3 is broader but has modal cells blue [1,0] and red [0,3]. Candidate records (k0,k1,k2) keep n0,n1,n3 peaks like current; for n2 each conditional command concentrates probability strongly on blue [0,2] and red [0,1], reducing the multimodality seen in the current view.

05. s1321_w02_t12_A_A_only_prospective_restored

Source: A; view: A_only; variant: prospective_restored.

Narrator transport complete: true; availability: retained_first_outcome; API status: completed.

Narrator text SHA-256: e725b1901403b6ba17a605ad9f87bbcb9bf4a39b0bc630b8fd34330f052e869d.

Narrator text:

Panel p0 contains probabilistic (fallible) location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3. Current distributions (represented_time 12 or 13 as noted in origins) are provided for each node: n0 (blue peak at cell [0,3]; red peak at [0,1]), n1 (blue [1,1]; red [0,3]), n2 (blue peaks at [0,2],[0,3],[1,2],[1,3]; red peaks at [0,0],[0,1],[1,0],[1,1]), and n3 (blue [1,0]; red [0,3]). Candidate conditional records (commands k0, k1, k2) supply alternative, conditional distributions for the same nodes; many candidate records match the current peaks at n0, n1 and n3, while candidate peaks at n2 are concentrated at [0,2] (blue) and [0,1] (red) in k0–k2. Origins: copied prior records are inherited (not new estimates) and conditional command records are conditional estimates (not events already executed). No physical presence, subjective state, or calibration is inferred; numbers report distributional peaks only.

Narrator text:

This packet presents fallible 4×4 location distributions (rows increase downward, columns increase rightward) for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Origins and times are attached: some records are copied prior records (not new estimates) and candidate command records are conditional estimates (not executed events). Current (non-conditional) estimates: in both panels n0 concentrates blue at (0,3) and red at (0,1); n1 concentrates blue at (1,1) and red at (0,3). For both panels current n2 is multimodal (blue maxima at (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3); red maxima at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)). Current n3 in both panels shows blue peaking at (1,0) in p0 and at the upper-left four cells in p1; red peaks differ by node as listed above. Candidate command records (k0, k1, k2) change some node distributions: notable changes occur at node n2 across commands and panels (different sets of maxima or a uniform candidate), while many other node peaks remain the same across conditional records.

Narrator text:

This packet contains fallible location distributions for red and blue roles on 4×4 local grids in panels p0 and p1. Panel p1 current n2 and n3 show the same multimodal peak groupings as p0 current n2/n3. Conditional command records change those multimodal patterns in several candidate branches: for example, p0 candidate k0 and k1 concentrate blue and red at single cells for node n2 (blue at (0,2) with higher peak probability in k1; red at (0,1) in those branches), while k2 yields coincident red and blue peaks at (0,1). Panel p1 candidates include both uniform (all 16 cells as peaks) and concentrated conditional forecasts for node n2 across different commands. No physical presence, event occurrence or calibrated confidence is asserted — the text reports only the supplied distribution peaks and the conditional vs current provenance.

- n0: blue peak at (0,3) (≈0.995); red peak at (0,1) (≈0.9995).

Other nodes (n0,n1,n3) keep the same peak cells across current and candidates.

Narrator text:

This packet contains fallible location distributions for red and blue roles on 4×4 local grids at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Current estimates (represented_time 12/13 as supplied) are reported per panel. In both panels, current n0 concentrates blue at (0,3) and red at (0,1); current n1 concentrates blue at (1,1) and red at (0,3). Current n3 places blue mass on the top-left 2×2 and red mass on the top-right 2×2. Current n2 in p0 and p1 differs by panel and origin in the packet: some records for n2 are uniform (all 16 cells equal) while other current entries place blue mass on the upper-right quadrant and red on the upper-left quadrant (see propositions). Origins: entries labeled copied_prior_record or completed_record were copied from earlier record_time(s) (not new estimates); entries labeled command_estimate or native_conditional_record are conditional-branch estimates (represented_time 13) and are not claims that a conditional branch executed. Candidate command records (k0,k1,k2) show how the same node’s distribution would differ under each conditional command; those differences are summarized by the peak locations in the propositions above.
